Next consider the case
\alpha=1/2
. To apply
Theorem

3
, we observe that

\ratio{}^{2}=\widehat{\mathbb{C}}^{2}_{2}/\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}=\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{4}/\left(\frac{4\delta_{1}^{2}u^{\top}\bar{\Sigma}_{2}u}{n_{2}}\right)=\frac{n_{2}\|\bar{\Sigma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}))\|^{2}}{4}.

We then have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}(1+\ratio)^{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|}{n_{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}^{1/2}(1+\ratio)}\right\}\bigg|D_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\langle X_{i}-\theta(P^{N}),u\rangle^{2}}{n_{2}u^{\top}\bar{\Sigma}_{2}u(1+\ratio)^{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\langle X_{i}-\theta(P^{N}),u\rangle|}{\sqrt{n_{2}u^{\top}\bar{\Sigma}_{2}u}(1+\ratio)}\right\}\bigg|D_{1}\right]\leq\frac{1}{(1+\ratio)^{2}}.

By
Theorem

3
and
Theorem

8
, we conclude concludes the result for
\alpha=1/2
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem

19
.

The result is a direct consequence of
Lemma

34
and
Lemma

35

∎

Lemma 34
.

When
\alpha=1/2
, for any
\varepsilon>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\!\left(\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{2}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,1/2}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq 2\sqrt{2}\varepsilon^{-1/2}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}\right\}\right)\geq 1-\varepsilon,

for
n_{1},n_{2}\geq 1
.

Proof of
Lemma

34
.

When
\alpha=1/2
, that is
z_{\alpha}=0
,
Theorem

16
establishes that the diameter of the confidence set can be computed exactly as

\displaystyle 2\|\widehat{H}\|_{2}\quad\textrm{where}\quad\widehat{H}=n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}Z_{i}-\widehat{\theta}_{1}.

By triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{H}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}+\left\|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}Z_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\right\|_{2}.

Observe that
\theta(P^{N})=\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}]
for all
1\leq i\leq N
, under independence, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P^{N}}\left[\left\|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}Z_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\E_{P^{N}}\left[\left\|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(Z_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[Z_{i}])\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}.

Denoting

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{N}=\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}+\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}},

it follows as

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}(2\|\widehat{H}\|_{2}\geq C\varepsilon^{-1/2}\mathrm{R}_{N})\leq\frac{4\varepsilon\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}[\|\widehat{H}\|_{2}^{2}/\mathrm{R}_{N}^{2}]}{C^{2}}\leq\frac{8\varepsilon}{C^{2}}\E_{P^{1}}\left[\frac{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}^{2}+\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})/n_{2}}{\mathrm{R}_{N}^{2}}\right]\leq\varepsilon,

with
C=2\sqrt{2}
. This concludes the result.
∎

Lemma 35
.

Let

\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}
be as in
(A5)
.
For any
\varepsilon>0
, setting
\varepsilon^{\circ}=\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\!\left(\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{2}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\left(1+|z_{\alpha}|\right)\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/2}\right\}\right)\geq 1-\varepsilon^{\circ},

provided
\max\{2,z_{\alpha}^{2}C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\}\leq n_{2}
where
C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
and
C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
depend on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
, but not on
d
or
\alpha
.

Proof of
Lemma

35
.

Throughout, we treat
\|\cdot\|\equiv\|\cdot\|_{2}
. The proof is a direct application of
Theorem

30
, and thus proceeds by verifying
(A1)
,
(A2)
, and
(A4)
to hold locally and
(A1-global)
,
(A2-ratio)
, and
(A4-ratio)
to hold globally.

Verifying
(A1)

For any
\theta\in\Theta
,

\displaystyle m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}:=\|X-\theta\|^{2}-\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}=2(X-\theta(P^{N}))^{\top}(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))+\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2},

and
\M_{2}(\theta)-\M_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))=\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}
. Thus
(A1)
holds with
\gamma=1
and
c_{0}=1
.

Verifying
(A2)

For any
\theta
such that
\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\leq\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\leq\delta}\,|m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}-\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})|

\displaystyle\quad=\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\leq\delta}\,2|\big(X-\theta(P^{N})\big)^{\top}(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)|

\displaystyle\quad=2\delta\sup_{u\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\,|u^{\top}(X-\theta(P^{N}))|

\displaystyle\quad=2\delta\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|.

Below, denote by
\{\epsilon_{i}\}_{i\in I_{2}}
independent Rademacher random variables. We then obtain by symmetrization, such that,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|<\delta}|(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\E_{P^{2}\times\epsilon}\left[\left|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}2\delta\epsilon_{i}\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4}{n_{2}}\left(\E_{P^{2}\times\epsilon}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\epsilon_{i}\delta\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|\right|^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{4}{n_{2}}\left(\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\delta^{2}\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\delta}{n_{2}}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta).

This satisfies the requirement with
q=1
.

Verifying
(A4)

First, we derive
\omega_{\mathtt{pop}}
. We have

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]

\displaystyle\quad=\E_{P_{i}}[(2(X-\theta)^{\top}(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))+\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2})^{2}]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 8\E_{P_{i}}|(X-\theta)^{\top}(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))|^{2}+2\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{4}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 8\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\E_{P_{i}}\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}+2\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{4}.

Hence we can set

\displaystyle\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(\delta)=\frac{8\delta^{2}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}+2\delta^{4}.

In order to derive
\omega_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(\delta)
, we use
Lemma

33
. From the earlier derivation, the local envelope function can be defined as

\displaystyle M_{\delta}(X_{i})

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\leq\delta}\,|(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{)}})(X_{i})|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\leq\delta}\,|(2(X_{i}-\theta(P^{N}))^{\top}(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))+\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|+\delta^{2}.

By invoking
Lemma

33
with
q=2
, we have for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|<\delta}\left|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 16\,\E_{P^{2}}[|M_{\delta}(X)|^{2}]+8\cdot 8^{1/2}\,n_{2}^{1/2}(\E_{P^{2}}[|M_{\delta}(X)|^{2}])^{1/2}\,\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 32(2\delta)^{2}\E_{P^{2}}[\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}]+32\delta^{4}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+8\cdot 8^{1/2}\,n_{2}^{1/2}2\delta(\E_{P^{2}}[\|X-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}])^{1/2}\,\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+8\cdot 8^{1/2}\,n_{2}^{1/2}\sqrt{2}\delta^{2}\,\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta)

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\left(\delta^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})+\delta^{3}\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}+\delta^{4}\right):=\omega^{2}_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(\delta),

for some universal constant
C>0
.

Notice that this derivation does not satisfy
q<1+\gamma
globally. Suppose that
\delta\leq\rho
, then

\displaystyle\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(\delta)

\displaystyle=C\left(\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})+\rho^{2}\right)\delta^{2},\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle\omega^{2}_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(\delta)

\displaystyle=C\left(\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})+\rho\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}+\rho^{2}\right)\delta^{2},

hence we can take
q=1
and
q<1+\gamma
is now satisfied locally.

Verifying
(A1-global)

(A1)
holds globally and thus one can choose

\displaystyle C_{\rho}(\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|)=\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}.

Verifying
(A2-ratio)

For any
\delta>\rho
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\left|\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))}{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4}{n_{2}\rho}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\frac{4\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}}{\sqrt{n_{2}}\rho}=R(n_{2},\rho).

Hence,
(A2-ratio)
holds with
C_{\mathtt{ratio}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{ratio}}
by Markov’s inequality.

Verifying
(A4-ratio)

Similarly for any
\delta>\rho
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\left|\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]}{\{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})z_{\alpha}^{2}}{\rho^{2}n_{2}}+\frac{C\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}z_{\alpha}^{2}}{\rho n_{2}}+\frac{Cz_{\alpha}^{2}}{n_{2}}=S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha),

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}(Z_{i})]}{n_{2}^{2}\{\M_{2}(\theta)-\M_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\leq\frac{Cz_{\alpha}^{2}}{n_{2}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{\rho^{2}}+1\right)=S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha).

Hence,
(A4-ratio)
holds with
\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{emp}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{emp}}
by Markov’s inequality.

Evaluating the rate of convergence

We now evaluate the rate of convergence by applying
Theorem

13
and
Theorem

30
. Denote by
C>0
a universal constant that changes from line to line. Choose
\rho=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}
. Then, using the fact that
c_{0}=1
and
\gamma=1
,

\displaystyle r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\phi_{n_{2}}(r_{n_{2}})\leq 1\Leftrightarrow Cr^{-2}_{n_{2}}r_{n_{2}}\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/2}\leq 1\Leftrightarrow C\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/2}\leq r_{n_{2}}.

Next, we evaluate the value related to
\omega_{\mathtt{pop}}
. This follows as

\displaystyle u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(u_{n_{2}})\leq n_{2}\Leftrightarrow Cu_{n_{2}}^{-2}\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})\leq n_{2}\Leftrightarrow C\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}\leq u_{n_{2}}.

Finally, the value related to
\omega_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}
yields that

\displaystyle u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\omega^{2}_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(u_{n_{2}})\leq n_{2}\Leftrightarrow Cu_{n_{2}}^{-4}\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})u_{n_{2}}^{2}\leq n_{2}\Leftrightarrow C\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}\leq u_{n_{2}}.

Then by
Theorem

13
, we obtain

\displaystyle(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1+\gamma-q}\mathrm{R}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N}

\displaystyle=(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}^{1/2}_{n_{1},n_{2}}.

Meanwhile, by
Theorem

30
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}=(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1/2}\widetilde{s}^{1/2}_{n_{1},n_{2}}

in view of
C_{\rho}(\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|)=\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}
. Hence, we conclude that

\displaystyle\max\{(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1/(1+\gamma-q)}\mathrm{R}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N},\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}\mathbf{1}\{\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}\geq\rho\}\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}^{1/2}_{n_{1},n_{2}},

with probability greater than
1-\varepsilon
as long as
n_{2}
is large enough such that

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon}\max\{R(n_{2},\rho),S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha),S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha)\}\leq 1/3,

where
C_{\varepsilon}
is a constant depending on
\varepsilon
.
This concludes the result.
∎

For
Corollary

19.1
, we instead prove the slightly rephrased version of the corollary.

Corollary 35.1
.

Suppose the initial estimator satisfies for all
n_{1}\geq N_{1}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{1}}\left(\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}\right)\geq 1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}},

For any
\varepsilon\in(0,1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}})
, setting
\varepsilon^{\circ}=\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
,
n_{1}\geq N_{1}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\!\left(\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{2}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq C^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\left(1+|z_{\alpha}|\right)\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}}\right\}\right)\geq 1-\varepsilon^{\circ},

provided
n_{2}\geq 1
when
\alpha=1/2
, and
n_{2}\geq\max\{2,z_{\alpha}^{2}C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\}
when
\alpha\neq 1/2
,
where
C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
and
C^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
depend on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
, but not on
d
or
\alpha
.

Proof of
Corollary

35.1
.

Verifying
(A5)
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}|P^{1}}\left[\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}\right]\leq\frac{4\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\E_{P_{i}}\|X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}}{n_{2}}=\frac{4\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}

and
\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}^{2}=\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{4}
. Consider the event

\displaystyle\Omega_{\mathtt{init}}:=\left\{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}\leq\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}\right\}.

Then on this event, we can take

\displaystyle\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{2}=\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{1}n_{2}}+\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}^{2}},

since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}|P^{1}}\left[\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}\right]+\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{4}\leq\max\{4\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}},\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}^{2}\}\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{2}

with probability greater than
1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
. Then the result follows with

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/4}+\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{2})}{n_{2}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Sigma}_{1})}{n_{1}}},

by AM-GM inequality.
∎

S.7.2

High-dimensional Misspecified Linear Regression

Proof of
Theorem

20
.

The proof is an application of
Theorem

3
for
\alpha=1/2
and
Theorem

8
for
\alpha\neq 1/2
. We collect relevant values. First,

\displaystyle\widehat{\xi}_{i}

\displaystyle=(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle=2\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}-2\E_{P_{i}}[\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\quad+\{(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2}-\E_{P_{i}}[\{X_{i}^{\top}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})\}^{2}|D_{1}].

Under non-identically distributed settings,
\E_{P_{i}}[\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}|D_{1}]
may not be zero.
Taking
G_{i}:=2\{X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}]\}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\E_{P_{i}}[(\widehat{\xi}_{i}-\langle\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}),G_{i}\rangle)^{2}|D_{1}]}{\E_{P_{i}}[\langle\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}),G_{i}\rangle^{2}|D_{1}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}[\{(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2}|D_{1}]}{4\E_{P_{i}}[\langle\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}),\{X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}]\}\rangle^{2}|D_{1}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\E_{P_{i}}[\{(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}\}^{4}|D_{1}]}{4\sigma^{2}_{i}\E_{P_{i}}[\langle\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}),X_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}]\rangle^{2}|D_{1}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L^{4}\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}}{4\sigma^{2}_{i}\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2})}.

Then the requirement of
Lemma

32
holds with

\displaystyle\varphi(\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}})=\frac{L^{4}\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}}{4\underline{\sigma}^{2}\underline{\lambda}}.

Denoting
\mathbb{V}_{G}=\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\langle u,X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}]\rangle^{2}]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{|\langle u,X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}]\rangle|^{2}}{\mathbb{V}_{G}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\langle u,X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}]\rangle|}{\mathbb{V}_{G}^{1/2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\sup_{u\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\langle W_{i}^{\circ},u\rangle^{2}}{n_{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\langle W_{i}^{\circ},u\rangle|}{\sqrt{n_{2}}}\right\}\right],

where

\bar{H}_{2}=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{P_{i}}(X_{i}\epsilon_{i})\quad\textrm{and}\quad W_{i}^{\circ}=\bar{H}_{2}^{-1/2}(X_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[X_{i}\epsilon_{i}]).

The result for
\alpha\neq 1/2
is concluded by applying
Lemma

32
.

For
\alpha=1/2
, we have an additional result from
Theorem

3
.
We obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}|D_{1}]\leq 4\E_{P_{i}}[\{\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2}|D_{1}]+2\E_{P_{i}}[\{(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}\}^{4}|D_{1}]

and hence

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\{u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\}^{2}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\{u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\}^{4}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}+\frac{2\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}L^{4}}{n_{2}}.

Meanwhile, the curvature is given by

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})^{\top}X_{i}|D_{1}]+\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\{X_{i}^{\top}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})\}^{2}|D_{1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\{X_{i}^{\top}(\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1})\}^{2}|D_{1}]=\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2},

where the second equality follows even under non-identically distributed settings. Finally, the following ratio is bounded from below as

2

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{2}\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}}{4\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}+2\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}L^{4}}=\frac{n_{2}\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}}{4\bar{\sigma}^{2}+2L^{4}\|\theta(P^{N})-\widehat{\theta}_{1}\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}}=\widetilde{\Delta}_{2}^{2}.

The remainder term in
Theorem

3
becomes

\displaystyle\E_{P^{1}}\left[\min\left\{1,C\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}(1+\ratio)^{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|}{n_{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}^{1/2}(1+\ratio)}\right\}\bigg|D_{1}\right]\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\E_{P^{1}}\left[\min\left\{1,\frac{C}{(1+\widetilde{\Delta}_{2})^{2}}\right\}\right].

This concludes the claim.

∎

Proof of
Theorem

21
.

The result is a direct consequence of
Lemma

36
and
Lemma

37

∎

Lemma 36
.

Assume
(B1)
and
(B2)
with
q_{x}>2
. For
\alpha=1/2
,
n_{1}\geq 1
, and any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\left(\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{\Gamma_{2}}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,1/2}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq 4\varepsilon^{-1/2}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\Gamma_{2}}\right\}\right)\geq 1-\varepsilon-\exp(-Cn_{2}),

provided that
n_{2}
satisfies
n_{2}\geq\mathfrak{C}d
where
\mathfrak{C}
depends only on
q_{x}
, and
C
is a universal constant.

Proof of
Lemma

36
.

When
\alpha=1/2
, that is
z_{\alpha}=0
,
Theorem

17
establishes that the diameter of the confidence set can be computed exactly, in terms of
\|\cdot\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}
,

\displaystyle 2\|\widehat{H}\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}\quad\textrm{where}\quad\widehat{H}=\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{\mathtt{OLS}}.

Here,
\theta_{\mathtt{OLS}}
is the OLS based on
D_{2}
. Throughout, we assume
\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}
is invertible, that is
n_{2}\geq d
. By the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{H}\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}+\|\theta_{\mathtt{OLS}}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}.

We observe

\displaystyle\theta_{\mathtt{OLS}}-\theta(P^{N})

\displaystyle=\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}Y_{i}\right)-\theta(P^{N})

\displaystyle=\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}(X^{\top}\theta(P^{N})+\varepsilon_{i})\right)-\theta(P^{N})

\displaystyle=\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}\right).

Hence, using
(B1)
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{N}}[\|\theta_{\mathtt{OLS}}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}]

\displaystyle=\E_{P^{N}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}\varepsilon_{i}\right)\right\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}^{2}\E_{P^{N}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}\right)\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\E_{P^{N}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\right)\right)\right]\leq\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}.

Also by linearity of expectation,

\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}[\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}_{2}}|D_{1}]=\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\Gamma_{2}}.

Denoting

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{N}=\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\Gamma_{2}},

it follows from Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\left(2\|\widehat{H}\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}}\geq C\varepsilon^{-1/2}\mathrm{R}_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\varepsilon}{C^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}\left[\frac{\|\widehat{H}\|^{2}_{\widehat{\Gamma}}}{\mathrm{R}_{N}^{2}}\right]\leq\frac{8\varepsilon}{C^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}\left[\frac{(d\bar{\sigma}^{2}/n_{2})+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\Gamma_{2}}^{2}}{\mathrm{R}_{N}^{2}}\right]\leq\varepsilon

where
C=2\sqrt{2}
. Finally, since

\displaystyle\|\widehat{H}\|^{2}_{\widehat{\Gamma}}=\widehat{H}^{\top}\widehat{\Gamma}\widehat{H}\geq\lambda_{\min}(\Gamma^{-1/2}\widehat{\Gamma}\Gamma^{-1/2})\cdot\|\widehat{H}\|^{2}_{\Gamma},

it remains to bound
\lambda_{\min}(\Gamma^{-1/2}\widehat{\Gamma}\Gamma^{-1/2})
from below. Note that
\Gamma^{-1/2}\widehat{\Gamma}\Gamma^{-1/2}=n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}
where
\widetilde{X}_{i}=\bar{\Gamma}^{-1/2}X_{i}
satisfies
n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}]=I_{d}
by construction. Theorem 1.3 of
Koltchinskii and Mendelson (2015)
implies that
(B2)
with
q_{x}>2
, whenever
d/n_{2}\leq\mathfrak{C}
for a universal constant
\mathfrak{C}
with probability at least
1-\exp(-C^{\prime}n_{2})
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\Gamma^{-1/2}\widehat{\Gamma}\Gamma^{-1/2})\geq 1/2\implies\|\widehat{H}\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}}\geq\|\widehat{H}\|_{\Gamma}/\sqrt{2}.

Therefore on this event, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\left(2\|\widehat{H}\|_{\Gamma}\geq\sqrt{2}C\varepsilon^{-1/2}\mathrm{R}_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\left(2\|\widehat{H}\|_{\widehat{\Gamma}}\geq C\varepsilon^{-1/2}\mathrm{R}_{N}\right)+\exp(-C^{\prime}n_{2})

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon+\exp(-C^{\prime}n_{2}).

This concludes the claim.
∎

Lemma 37
.

Assume
(B1)
,
(B2)
with
q_{x}\geq 4
, and
(B3)
and let

\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}
be as in
(A5)
. For
\alpha\neq 1/2
,
n_{1}\geq 1
, and any
\varepsilon>0
, setting
\varepsilon^{\circ}=\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
, it holds that

\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}\left(\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{2}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}}+\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/2}\right\}\right)\geq 1-\varepsilon^{\circ},

provided that
n_{2}
satisfies

C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\max\bigg\{((1+|z_{\alpha}|)d\log(2d)L^{4})^{q_{x}/(q_{x}-2)},((1+|z_{\alpha}|)(1+K)dL^{2})^{p/(p-1)}\bigg\}\leq n_{2}.

where
p=\min\{q_{y},q_{x}/2\}
, and
C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}},C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
depend on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
, but not on
d
or
\alpha
.

Proof of
Lemma

37
.

The proof is a direct application of
Theorem

30
and thus proceeds by verifying

(A1)
,
(A2)
, and
(A4)
to hold locally and
(A1-global)
,
(A2-ratio)
, and
(A4-ratio)
to hold globally.

Verifying
(A1)

For any
\theta\in\Theta
,

\displaystyle m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}

\displaystyle=(Y_{i}-\theta^{\top}X_{i})^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle=2\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}X_{i}+\{(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[2\varepsilon_{i}(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}X_{i}\right]+\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\{(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\{(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}X_{i}\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\right]\right)(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)=\|\theta(P^{N})-\theta\|^{2}_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}.

Thus
(A1)
holds with
\gamma=1
and
c_{0}=1
.

Verifying
(A2)

For any
\theta
such that
\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta}|(Y-\theta^{\top}X)^{2}-(Y-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)^{2}|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta}\left\{2|\varepsilon(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X-\theta^{\top}X)|+|(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X-\theta^{\top}X)^{2}|\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta}\left\{2|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon X|\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta}\left\{|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X|^{2}\right\}.

By Cauchy-Schwarz, for any
\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta
,

\displaystyle|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon X|

\displaystyle\leq|\varepsilon|\cdot\|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\|_{2}\cdot\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\varepsilon|\cdot\|\theta(P^{N})-\theta\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\cdot\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta|\varepsilon|\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2},

and

\displaystyle|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X|^{2}\leq\|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{2}\leq\delta^{2}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{2}.

For a measurable function
f
, we denote

\displaystyle(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})f=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}f(X_{i},Y_{i})-\E_{P_{i}}[f(X_{i},Y_{i})].

Then we have

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}<\delta}|(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))|\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\E_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}<\delta}|(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\delta\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[\varepsilon X]\|\right]+\delta^{2}\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[XX^{\top}]\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II},

where
\|\cdot\|_{\mathrm{op}}
denotes the operator norm.

Lemma

38
and
Lemma

39
at the end of this section prove that

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbf{II}\leq\left\{4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right)\right\}\delta^{2},

under
(B2)
and
(B1)
.

Notice that this derivation does not satisfy
q<1+\gamma
globally. Assume that
\delta\leq\rho
, then

\displaystyle\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta)=\left(2\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\left\{4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right)\right\}\rho\right)\delta,

hence we can take
q=1
and
q<1+\gamma
is now satisfied locally.

Verifying
(A4)

We begin by analyzing
\omega_{\mathtt{pop}}(\cdot)
. It follows that

\displaystyle(m_{\theta}(X_{i},Y_{i})-m_{\theta(P)}(X_{i},Y_{i}))^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 4|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon X|^{2}+2|(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X|^{4},

and hence

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}\leq\delta}\,\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P)})^{2}(X_{i},Y_{i})

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\delta^{2}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}\cdot|v\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X|^{2}\right]+\frac{2\delta^{4}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[|v\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X|^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2\delta^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}v^{\top}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\right]v+\frac{2\delta^{4}}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}L^{4}

\displaystyle\quad=2\delta^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}+2\delta^{4}L^{4}.

When
\delta\leq\rho
, we have

\displaystyle 2\delta^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}+2\delta^{4}L^{4}\leq\delta^{2}\left(2\bar{\sigma}^{2}+2\rho^{2}L^{4}\right)=\omega_{\mathtt{pop}}^{2}(\delta),

which satisfies
q=1
and
q<1+\gamma
locally.

Next, we derive
\omega_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(\cdot)
. By
Lemma

33
with
q=p=\min\{q_{y},q_{x}/2\}
, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}[|M_{\delta}|^{p}]\leq 2\cdot 2^{p-1}\delta^{p}\E_{P^{2}}[|\varepsilon|^{p}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{p}]+2^{p-1}\delta^{2p}\E_{P^{2}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{2p}].

We observe that

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}[|\varepsilon|^{p}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{p}]

\displaystyle=\E_{P^{2}}[\E_{P^{2}}[|\varepsilon|^{p}|X]\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq\E_{P^{2}}[\left(\E_{P^{2}}[|\varepsilon|^{q_{y}}|X]\right)^{p/q_{y}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq K^{p}\E_{P^{2}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq(\E_{P^{2}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{q_{x}}])^{p/q_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq K^{p}d^{p/2}L^{p}

assuming
p\leq q_{y}
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{2p}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\E_{P^{2}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X\|_{2}^{q_{x}}]\right)^{(2p)/q_{x}}\leq d^{p}L^{2p}

assuming
2p\leq q_{x}
. Hence by
Lemma

33
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}^{*}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|<\delta}\left|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 16\,n_{2}^{2/p-1}(2^{2/p}\cdot 2^{2-2/p}\delta^{2}K^{2}dL^{2}+2^{2-2/p}\delta^{4}d^{2}L^{4})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+8\cdot 8^{1/p}\,n_{2}^{1/p}(2^{1/p}\cdot 2^{1-1/p}\delta Kd^{1/2}L+2^{1-1/p}\delta^{2}dL^{2})(\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II})

\displaystyle\quad=A_{2}\delta^{2}+A_{3}\delta^{3}+A_{4}\delta^{4}

for
p=\min\{q_{y},q_{x}/2\}
where

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=C_{1}n_{2}^{2/p-1}K^{2}dL^{2}+n_{2}^{1/p}\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}KdL

\displaystyle A_{3}

\displaystyle=C_{2}\cdot n_{2}^{1/p}Kd^{1/2}L\left\{4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right)\right\},\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle A_{4}

\displaystyle=C_{3}n^{2/p-1}d^{2}L^{4}+8\cdot 4\,n_{2}^{1/p}dL^{2}\left\{4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right)\right\}.

When
\delta\leq\rho
, we can set
(A_{2}+A_{3}\rho+A_{4}\rho^{2})\delta^{2}
, which satisfies
q=1
and
q<1+\gamma
locally.

Verifying
(A1-global)

(A1)
holds globally and thus one can choose

\displaystyle C_{\rho}(\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|)=\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}.

Verifying
(A2-ratio)

Next, we have

\displaystyle\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\left|\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))}{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\rho^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\left\{4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right)\right\}=R(n_{2},\rho).

Hence,
(A2-ratio)
holds with
C_{\mathtt{ratio}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{ratio}}
by Markov’s inequality.

Verifying
(A4-ratio)

Similarly for any
\delta>\rho
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}^{*}\left[\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\left|\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]}{\{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{A_{2}(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})}{\rho^{2}n_{2}}+\frac{A_{3}(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})}{\rho n_{2}}+\frac{A_{4}(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})}{n_{2}}=S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha),

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|>\rho}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}(Z_{i})]}{n_{2}^{2}\{\M_{2}(\theta)-\M_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\leq\frac{(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})}{n_{2}}\left(2\rho^{-2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}d+2L^{4}\right)=S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha).

Hence,
(A4-ratio)
holds with
\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{emp}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{emp}}
by Markov’s inequality.

We assume that
n_{2}
is large enough satisfies the following:

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\max\{R(n_{2},\rho),S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha),S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha)\}\leq 1/3,

(E.13)

where
C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
is a constant depending on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
. We choose
\rho=C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\sqrt{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d/n_{2}}
with
C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
being a sufficiently large constant depending on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
. Then (
E.13
) is satisfied if

\begin{split}&(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\max\left\{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}},\frac{(1+K)dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-1/p}}\right\}\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\\
&\quad\Leftrightarrow C^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\max\left\{((1+|z_{\alpha}|)d\log(2d)L^{4})^{q_{x}/(q_{x}-2)},((1+|z_{\alpha}|)(1+K)dL^{2})^{p/(p-1)}\right\}\leq n_{2}.\end{split}

(E.14)

Evaluating the rate of convergence

We now evaluate the rate of convergence by applying
Theorem

13
and
Theorem

30
. Denote
\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
a constant depending on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
that changes from line to line. Then with the choice of
\rho
and
n_{2}
satisfying (
E.14
),

\displaystyle r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\phi_{n_{2}}(r_{n})\leq 1\Leftrightarrow r_{n_{2}}^{-1}\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}\leq 1\Leftrightarrow\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}\leq r_{n_{2}}.

Next, we evaluate the value related to
\omega_{\mathtt{pop}}
. Under the choice of
\rho
and
n_{2}
satisfying (
E.14
),

\displaystyle u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(u_{n_{2}})\leq n_{2}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow u_{n_{2}}^{-2}\left(2\bar{\sigma}^{2}\rho^{-2}+2L^{4}\right)\rho^{2}\leq n_{2}\Leftrightarrow\frac{\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}\leq u_{n_{2}}^{2},

where we used
\bar{\sigma}\leq\bar{\sigma}d
and
L^{4}/n_{2}\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}/(d\log(2d)n^{2/q_{x}})\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
by (
E.14
).

Finally, we evaluate the value related to
\omega_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}
. From earlier derivation, we have

\displaystyle(A_{2}+A_{3}\rho+A_{4}\rho^{2})\delta^{2}=n_{2}\rho^{2}\left(\frac{A_{2}}{n_{2}\rho^{2}}+\frac{A_{3}}{n_{2}\rho}+\frac{A_{4}}{n_{2}}\right)\delta^{2}\leq\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\bar{\sigma}^{2}d\delta^{2}=\omega^{2}_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}(\delta),

under (
E.14
). Hence we conclude

\displaystyle u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(u_{n_{2}})\leq n_{2}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\frac{\widetilde{C}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}\leq u_{n_{2}}^{2}.

By
Theorem

13
, we conclude

\displaystyle(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1+\gamma-q}\mathrm{R}_{N}^{\mathtt{CLT}}=C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}}+(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/2}.

Meanwhile, by
Theorem

30
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}=(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1/2}\widetilde{s}^{1/2}_{n_{1},n_{2}}

in view of
C_{\rho}(\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|)=\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}
. Hence, we conclude that

\displaystyle\max\{(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{1/(1+\gamma-q)}\mathrm{R}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N},\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}\mathbf{1}\{\mathrm{Q}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha}\geq\rho\}\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}}+(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}^{1/2}_{n_{1},n_{2}},

which concludes the result.

∎

For
Corollary

21.1
, we instead prove the slightly rephrased version of the corollary.

Corollary 37.1
.

Suppose the initial estimator satisfies, for all
n_{1}\geq N_{1}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{1}}\left(\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}\leq\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{1}}\right)\geq 1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}.

Assume
(B1)
and
(B2)
with
q_{x}>2
when
\alpha=1/2
and
q_{x}\geq 4
when
\alpha\neq 1/2
. Additionally assume
(B3)
when
\alpha\neq 1/2
.
For any
\varepsilon\in(0,1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}})
, setting
\varepsilon^{\circ}=\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
,
n_{1}\geq N_{1}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Diam}_{\|\cdot\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}}\bigl(\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}\bigr)\leq C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{2}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}d}{n_{1}}}\lambda^{1/2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})\right\}

with probability at least
1-\varepsilon^{\circ}-\exp(-Cn_{2})
,
provided
n_{2}\geq\mathfrak{C}d
when
\alpha=1/2
, and with probability
1-\varepsilon^{\circ}
provided that
n_{2}
satisfies

C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\max\bigg\{((1+|z_{\alpha}|)d\log(2d)L^{4})^{q_{x}/(q_{x}-2)},((1+|z_{\alpha}|)(1+K)dL^{2})^{p/(p-1)}\bigg\}\leq n_{2},

when
\alpha\neq 1/2
, where
C
is a universal constant,
p=\min\{q_{y},q_{x}/2\}
,
\mathfrak{C}
depends on
q_{x}>2
,
C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
and
C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
depend on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
, but not on
d
or
\alpha
.

Proof of
Corollary

37.1
.

Verifying
(A5)
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}|P^{1}}\left[\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}\right]+\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}+\frac{2\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}L^{4}}{n_{2}}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}+C\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})+C\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}^{4}\lambda^{2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})

since we already assumed that
CL^{4}\leq n_{2}
in (
E.14
). Consider the event

\displaystyle\Omega_{\mathtt{init}}:=\left\{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}\leq\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{1}}\right\}.

Then on this event, we can take

\displaystyle\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{2}=\frac{(d\bar{\sigma}^{2})(d\bar{\sigma}^{2})}{n_{1}n_{2}}\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})+\frac{(d\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}{n_{1}^{2}}\lambda^{2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}),

since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}|P^{1}}\left[\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}\right]+\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})+C\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|_{\bar{\Gamma}_{1}}^{4}\lambda^{2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\max\{\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}},\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{init}}^{2}\}\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{2}

with probability greater than
1-\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{\mathtt{init}}
. Then the result follows with

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\left(\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/4}\lambda^{1/4}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})+\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{1}}}\lambda^{1/2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}+\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{1}}}\lambda^{1/2}_{\max}(\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{1}^{-1/2})

by AM-GM inequality.
∎

Lemma 38
.

Let
X_{i}
and
\varepsilon_{i}=Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}
for
i\in I_{2}
. Then under
(B1)
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[\varepsilon X]\|\right]\leq\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}.

Proof of
Lemma

38
.

We have

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[\varepsilon X]\|_{2}\right]\leq\sqrt{\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}[\varepsilon X]\|_{2}^{2}\right]}.

We focus on the expectation inside, and obtain

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}[\varepsilon X]\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\E_{P^{2}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon_{i}X_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon_{i}X_{i}]\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]=\E_{P^{2}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}^{\circ}\right\|^{2}\right]

where

\displaystyle X_{i}^{\circ}=\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon_{i}X_{i}-\E_{P_{i}}[\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon_{i}X_{i}].

Since each
X_{i}^{\circ}
is mean zero, and as
X_{i}^{\circ}
and
X_{j}^{\circ}
are independent for
i\neq j
, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}X_{i}^{\circ}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\|X_{i}^{\circ}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathrm{tr}(\E_{P_{i}}[\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}])

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\bar{\sigma}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\E_{P_{i}}[\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}])

\displaystyle=\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}\cdot\mathrm{tr}(\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2})=\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}},

where we used
(B1)
with the tower property of the expectation. This concludes that

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[\varepsilon X]\|\right]\leq\sqrt{\frac{d\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{n_{2}}}.

∎

Lemma 39
.

Let
X_{i}
for
i\in I_{2}
. Then under
(B2)
for
q_{x}\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[XX^{\top}]\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\right]\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{d\log(2d)L^{4}}{n_{2}}}+32\sqrt{2}\log(2d)\left(\frac{dL^{2}}{n_{2}^{1-2/q_{x}}}\right).

Proof of
Lemma

39
.

We use Theorem I of
Tropp (2016)
to bound the expectation of the operator norm. Define

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(X)=\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}\left[\left(\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}-\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}^{2}=\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\left(\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}-\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}\right].

Then Theorem I of
Tropp (2016)
states that

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}(\mathbb{P}_{n_{2}}-P^{2})[XX^{\top}]\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\right]\leq 2\sqrt{(1+2\log(2d))\mathbf{v}(X)}+4(1+2\log(2d))\mathbf{L}.

Now we derive bounds for
\mathbf{v}(X)
and
\mathbf{L}
. We begin with
\mathbf{v}(X)
. First denote

\displaystyle\widetilde{X}_{i}=\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\quad\textrm{and}\quad\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}=\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}\left[\left(\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}-\frac{\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}}{n_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]=\E_{P_{i}}\left[\left(\frac{\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}}-\frac{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}}{n_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}}{n_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right]=\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{X}_{i}\|^{2}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right]-\frac{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(X)=\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{X}_{i}\|^{2}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right]-\frac{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{X}_{i}\|^{2}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}.

Now, we focus on the expectation inside. For any
\|u\|_{2}=1
,

\displaystyle u^{\top}\E_{P_{i}}[\|\widetilde{X}_{i}\|^{2}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}]u

\displaystyle=\E_{P_{i}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{2}|u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\E_{P_{i}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{4}}\sqrt{\E_{P_{i}}|u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{4}}.

We observe that

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{4}]

\displaystyle=\E_{P_{i}}\left[\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}(e_{j}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i})^{2}\right\}^{2}\right]\leq d^{2}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{1}{d}\sum_{j=1}^{d}(e_{j}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i})^{4}\right]\leq d^{2}L^{4},

where the last step follows by
(B2)
since
q_{x}\geq 4
. Similarly, we have
\E_{P_{i}}|u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{4}\leq L^{4}
. Putting together

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(X)\leq\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{X}_{i}\|^{2}\widetilde{X}_{i}\widetilde{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n_{2}^{2}}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\frac{dL^{4}}{n_{2}}.

Next for
\mathbf{L}
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{n_{2}^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{4}\right]+\frac{2}{n_{2}^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}\right].

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{q_{x}}\right]\right)^{4/q_{x}}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{q_{x}}]\right)^{4/q_{x}}.

As before, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}\left[\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{q_{x}}\right]

\displaystyle=\E_{P_{i}}\left[\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}(e_{j}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i})^{2}\right\}^{q_{x}/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq d^{q_{x}/2}\E_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{1}{d}\sum_{j=1}^{d}|e_{j}^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{q_{x}}\right]\leq d^{q_{x}/2}L^{q_{x}}

where the last step follows by
(B2)
. Therefore, we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P^{2}}\left[\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}\|^{4}\right]\leq n_{2}^{4/q_{x}}d^{2}L^{4}.

For
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\|\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\|u\|=1}u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}\Gamma_{i}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}u=\sup_{\|u\|=1}\E_{P_{i}}[|u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{2}]\leq\sup_{\|u\|=1}\big(\E_{P_{i}}[|u^{\top}\bar{\Gamma}_{2}^{-1/2}X_{i}|^{4}]\big)^{1/2}\leq L^{2}.

Putting together

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}\leq\sqrt{2}n_{2}^{-1+2/q_{x}}dL^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}L^{2}}{n_{2}}\leq 2\sqrt{2}n_{2}^{-1+2/q_{x}}dL^{2}.

The result is concluded by choosing
\eta=1
and plugging
\mathbf{v}(X)
and
\mathbf{L}
into the expression of Theorem I of
Tropp (2016)
.

∎

Proof of
.

Since the additional term
\lambda(\theta)
is not random, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\xi}_{i}

\displaystyle=(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X)^{2}+\lambda(\widehat{\theta})-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)^{2}-\lambda(\theta(P^{N}))

\displaystyle\quad-\E_{P_{i}}[(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X)^{2}+\lambda(\widehat{\theta})-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)^{2}-\lambda(\theta(P^{N}))|D_{1}]

\displaystyle=(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X)^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)^{2}-\E_{P_{i}}[(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X)^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)^{2}|D_{1}].

Thus the expression for
\widehat{\xi}_{i}
is identical as in
Theorem

20
. Hence the validity result by
Theorem

20
remains to hold whenever
\widehat{\theta}_{1}
is consistent. For
\alpha=1/2
, the upper bound for variance also does not change since
\lambda(\theta)
is constant. The expression for
\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}
may depend on
\lambda(\cdot)
. In particular, for any convex
\lambda
and
g_{0}\in\partial\lambda(\theta_{0})
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[(Y_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X_{i})^{2}-(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}+\lambda(\widehat{\theta})-\lambda(\theta(P^{N}))|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[2(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X_{i})+g_{0}^{\top}(\widehat{\theta}-\theta(P^{N}))|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[(\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}|D_{1}].

On the other hand, since
\theta(P^{N})
is a population minimizer, it follows that

\displaystyle 0\in\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[2(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})X_{i}]+\partial\lambda(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\implies\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[2(Y_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}-\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X_{i})+g_{0}^{\top}(\widehat{\theta}-\theta(P^{N}))|D_{1}]=0.

Hence the lower bound

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{C}}_{2}\geq\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[(\widehat{\theta}^{\top}X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})^{2}|D_{1}]

remains to hold when
\lambda(\cdot)
is convex. The rest of the proof is identical to that of
Theorem

20
.
∎

S.7.3

Manski’s Discrete Choice Model

In this example, we take

m_{\theta}:=(y,x)\mapsto-\frac{1}{2}\,y\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}x).

whose envelop function for
m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}
is trivially given by a constant function at
1
. The leading constant
1/2
is introduced without loss of generality.

Proof of
Theorem

23
.

The proof is an application of
Theorem

3
for
\alpha=1/2
and
Theorem

8
for
\alpha\neq 1/2
. First, observe that

\displaystyle\left|\E_{P_{i}}\left[-\frac{1}{2}\,Y_{i}\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})+\frac{1}{2}\,Y_{i}\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|\E_{P_{i}}\left[(2\eta_{P_{i}}(X_{i})-1)\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i}))=d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N})).

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}]=\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}\left[\frac{1}{2}Y_{i}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})-\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)-\left[\frac{1}{2}\E_{P_{i}}\left[Y_{i}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})-\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\right]\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)-\left\{\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\left\{1-\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))(1-d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))).

Finally, using the fact that
|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}[|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{3}]

\displaystyle\leq\E_{P_{i}}[|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\E_{P_{i}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{2}\,Y_{i}\cdot\left\{\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})-\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\right\}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\right)=d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N})).

Putting together, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}\left[\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{2}}{\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|}{\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}^{1/2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}\left[\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{3}}{\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}^{3/2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P^{1}}\left[\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}|d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))|}{\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))(1-d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N})))\right)^{3/2}}\right].

First, assume that
d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\leq 1/2
for all
i\in I_{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\min\left\{1,C\mathbb{E}_{P^{1}}\left[\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}|d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))|}{\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))(1-d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N})))\right)^{3/2}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\min\left\{1,C\mathbb{E}_{P^{1}}\left[\frac{2^{3/2}}{\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\right)^{1/2}}\right]\right\}.

Meanwhile if
d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\geq 1/2
for some
i\in I_{2}
, then
\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\geq 1/2
, and the result holds trivially. Finally, in view of
(B5)
and
Theorem

8
, we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P^{N}}(\theta(P^{N})\not\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}^{\mathtt{CLT}}_{N,\alpha})\leq\alpha+\E_{P^{1}}\left[\min\left\{1,\frac{C}{(n_{2}\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|)^{1/2}}\right\}\right],

where
C>0
is a constant depending on
c_{1}
.

Next, consider the case
\alpha=1/2
. We derive the lower bound on the curvature. Following Proposition 1 of
Tsybakov (2004)
(and similarly for Proposition 2.4 of
Mukherjee et al. (2021)
), we define the set
\mathcal{A}(\theta):=\{x:\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X)\}
for each
\theta
. It then follows that

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\E_{P_{i}}\left[Y\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)-\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{A}(\theta)}\,|\E_{P_{i}}[Y|X=x]|P_{X}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{A}(\theta)}\,|\eta_{P_{i}}(x)-1/2|P_{X}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\geq 2\sup_{0\leq t\leq t^{*}}t\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(|\eta_{P_{i}}(X)-1/2|\geq t\cap X\in\mathcal{A}(\theta)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 2\sup_{0\leq t\leq t^{*}}t\left(d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))-\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(|\eta_{P}(X)-0.5|\leq t\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 2\sup_{0\leq t\leq t^{*}}t\left(d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))-C_{0}t^{1/\gamma}\right).

The last inequality uses
(B4)
. The optimal choice of
t
is given by

\displaystyle t=\begin{cases}(1+1/\gamma)^{-\gamma}C_{0}^{-\gamma}d^{\gamma}_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))&\text{when}\quad d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\leq(1+1/\gamma)C_{0}(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}\\
t^{*}&\text{otherwise.}\end{cases}

Putting together, it follows for all
\theta\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
,

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{2}{1+\gamma}\right)\frac{d^{1+\gamma}_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))}{(1+1/\gamma)^{\gamma}C_{0}^{\gamma}}\mathbf{1}\left\{d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\leq(1+1/\gamma)C_{0}(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{2}{1+\gamma}\right)t^{*}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\left\{d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))>(1+1/\gamma)C_{0}(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\mathfrak{C}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\min\{d^{\gamma}_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N})),t^{*}\},

where
\mathfrak{C}
depends on
C_{0}
. Based on the earlier calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}]\leq\E_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}]\leq d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N})).

Hence, we have

2

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathfrak{C}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\min\{d^{\gamma}_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N})),t^{*}\})^{2}}{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathfrak{C}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\min\{c_{1}\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{\gamma},t^{*}\})^{2}}{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))}

\displaystyle\geq\mathfrak{C}^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\min\{c_{1}^{2}\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2\gamma},(t^{*})^{2}\}

\displaystyle\geq\mathfrak{C}n_{2}\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|\min\{\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2\gamma},(t^{*})^{2}\}=\widetilde{\Delta}_{2}^{2},

where we used
(B5)
and
\mathfrak{C}
depends on
C_{0}
and
c_{1}
.

Finally by
Theorem

3
, we have

\displaystyle\min\left\{1,C\mathbb{E}_{P^{N}}\left[\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}(1+\ratio)^{2}}\min\left\{1,\,\frac{|\widehat{\xi}_{i}|}{n_{2}\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{2}^{1/2}(1+\ratio)}\right\}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\min\left\{1,C\E_{P^{1}}\left[\frac{1}{(1+\widetilde{\Delta}_{2})^{2}}\right]\right\}.

This concludes the claim.
∎

Proof of
Theorem

24
.

The proof is a direct application of
Theorem

30
and thus proceeds by verifying
(A1)
,
(A2)
,
(A4)
, to hold locally and
(A1-global)
,
(A2-ratio)
, and
(A4-ratio)
to hold globally. We first establish the convergence rate of the diameter in terms of the pseudo-metric
d_{\Delta}(\theta_{1},\theta_{2})
and then translate the result to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
. For identical distributions
X_{i}
with
i\in I_{2}
, we define the metric over
P^{2}
as

\displaystyle d_{\Delta}(\theta_{1},\theta_{2})=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta_{1},\theta_{2}).

We define

\displaystyle\M_{2}(\theta)=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\,Y_{i}\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X_{i})\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\widehat{\M}_{2}(\theta)=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\,Y_{i}\cdot\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X_{i})\right).

We consider the following collection of “localized” functions:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\delta}^{\Delta}:=\left\{\frac{1}{2}\,y\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x)-\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}x)\right)\text{ for all }\theta\text{ s.t., }d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\leq\delta\text{ and }\theta\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\right\}.

Verifying
(A1)

Throughout, we assume
X_{i}
is identically distributed. In the proof of
Theorem

23
, we have already established that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})\geq\mathfrak{C}d^{1+\gamma}_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))

whenever
d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\leq(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}
where
\mathfrak{C}
depends on
C_{0},\gamma
. Hence
(A1)
holds locally with
\gamma
for
d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))\leq(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}
.

Verifying
(A2)

For a measurable function
f
, we denote

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{n_{2}}f=n_{2}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}f(X_{i},Y_{i})-\E_{P_{i}}[f(X_{i},Y_{i})]\right).

(E.15)

Then

\displaystyle\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))<\delta}|(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))|\right]

\displaystyle\quad=n_{2}^{-1/2}\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))<\delta}|\mathbb{G}_{n_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})|\right]=n_{2}^{-1/2}\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}^{\Delta}_{\delta}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n_{2}}m\right|\right].

In order to control this term, we introduce the following objects. For any set
\Theta
equipped with a metric
\|\cdot\|
, and any
\varepsilon>0
, an
\varepsilon
-covering number
\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon,\Theta,\|\cdot\|)
of
\Theta
relative to the metric
\|\cdot\|
is defined as the minimal number of
\|\cdot\|
-balls of radius less than or equal to
\varepsilon
required for covering
\Theta
. In particular, we consider when
\Theta
contains measurable functions of observations
Z_{i}\in\mathcal{Z}
and let
Q
be any discrete probability measure on
Z_{i}
for
i\in I_{2}
. We define an envelop function
F
of the class
\Theta
as
F:=z\mapsto\sup_{f\in\Theta}|f(z)|
. The uniform entropy numbers of
\Theta
relative to
L_{r}
is defined as

\displaystyle J(\delta,\Theta,L_{r}):=\sup_{Q}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{1+\log\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon\|F\|_{Q,r},\Theta,L_{r}(Q))}\,d\varepsilon

where
\|f\|_{Q,r}:=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}f^{r}(z_{i})Q(z_{i})\right)^{1/r}
.
We use the following result from
van der Vaart and Wellner (2011)
:

Theorem 40

(Theorem 2.1 of
van der Vaart and Wellner (2011)
)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}
be a collection of
P
-square integrable functions equipped with an envelop function
F\leq 1
. If
\mathbb{E}_{P}f^{2}\leq t^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}F^{2}
, for every
f
and some
t\in(0,1)
, then

\displaystyle\E_{P}^{*}\,\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\,|\mathbb{G}_{n}f|\right]\lesssim J(t,\mathcal{F},L_{2})\left(1+\frac{J(t,\mathcal{F},L_{2})}{t^{2}\sqrt{n}\|F\|_{P,2}}\right)\|F\|_{P,2}.

First, we relate the covering number of
\mathcal{M}^{\Delta}_{\delta}
to the VC dimension of the subgraphs of the functions in
\mathcal{M}^{\Delta}_{\delta}
. First, the function
f
in
\mathcal{M}^{\Delta}_{\delta}
takes values in
\{-1,0,1\}
. Hence, for the subgraph
\{(x,y,t):t<f(x,y)\}
, we only need to consider cases with
-1<t\leq 0
and
0<t\leq 1
. They are identical and only provide the case with
0<t\leq 1
. We only need to consider the set where the function evaluates to non-zero values, that is,

\displaystyle\left\{(1,x,t):\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x>0,\theta^{\top}x\leq 0,t>0\right\}\cup\left\{(-1,x,t):\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x\leq 0,\theta^{\top}x>0,t>0\right\}.

Then,

\displaystyle\left\{(y,x,t)\,:\,2^{-1}y\{\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x)-\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}x)\}\geq t,t>0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\subseteq\left\{(1,x,t):\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x>0,\theta^{\top}x\leq 0,t>0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\cup\left\{(-1,x,t):\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x\leq 0,\theta^{\top}x>0,t>0\right\}.

Each component is the intersection of two half-spaces in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
whose VC-dimension is
d+1
and the intersection of two sets in VC-classes is also VC. The corresponding VC-dimension of the resulting set space is
2d+2
. See Lemma 2.6.17 of
van der Vaart and Wellner (1996)
. Since the subgraph of a function is VC, the covering number of the function space is

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon\|M\|_{L_{2}(Q)},\mathcal{M}_{\delta}^{\Delta},L_{2}(Q))\leq Cd(16e)^{d}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)^{4d}

by Theorem 2.6.7 of
van der Vaart and Wellner (1996)
with their
r=2
for any probability measure
Q
and
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
and
C
is a universal constant. We thus obtain

\displaystyle t\mapsto J(t,\mathcal{M}^{\Delta}_{\delta},L_{2})

\displaystyle=\sup_{Q}\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{1+\log\left(Cd(16e)^{d}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)^{4d}\right)}\,d\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{Q}\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\mathfrak{C}d+4d\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)}\,d\varepsilon\leq\mathfrak{C}t\sqrt{d\log(1/t)}

where
\mathfrak{C}
is a universal constants that may change line by line.

Next, for the variance bound, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}f^{2}=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}x)\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}x)\right)=\delta.

Thus the condition of Theorem
40
holds with
t^{2}=\delta
and
F=1
. By Theorem
40
, we obtain

\displaystyle\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\,\left[\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{\delta}^{\Delta}}\,|\mathbb{G}_{n_{2}}m|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim J(\sqrt{\delta},\mathcal{F},L_{2})\left(1+\frac{J(\sqrt{\delta},\mathcal{F},L_{2})}{\delta\sqrt{n_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\delta d\log(1/\delta)}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{\delta d\log(1/\delta)}}{\delta\sqrt{n_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\delta d\log(1/\delta)}+\frac{d\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n_{2}}}.

We can thus take
\phi_{n_{2}}
in
(A2)
as

\displaystyle\delta\mapsto\phi_{n_{2}}(\delta)=C\left(\sqrt{\frac{\delta d\log(1/\delta)}{n_{2}}}+\frac{d\log(1/\delta)}{n_{2}}\right),

where
C
is a universal constant. The requirement
q<1+\gamma
is satisfies with
q=1/2
.

Verifying
(A4)

Recalling the discussion after
Theorem

13
, we have
\phi_{n_{2}}=\omega_{n_{2},\mathtt{emp}}
for bounded processes. This follows from the contraction principle
(
Ledoux and Talagrand, 2013
, Theorem 4.12)
. Thus we only need to evaluate
\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}
. This follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}(Z_{i})]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})\right)=\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\theta,\theta(P^{N})).

Hence
\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(\delta)=\delta
, which satisfies the requirement
q<1+\gamma
with
q=1/2
.

Verifying
(A1-global)

Setting
\delta>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}
, we have shown that

\displaystyle C_{\delta_{0}}(d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N})))\geq\mathfrak{C}t^{*}d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))

from the proof of
Theorem

23
.

Verifying
(A2-ratio)

For any
\delta>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\E^{*}_{P^{2}}\left[\sup_{d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}}\left|\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta)-(\widehat{\mathbb{M}}_{2}-\mathbb{M}_{2})(\theta(P^{N}))}{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\left(\sqrt{\frac{d\log(1/t^{*})}{\gamma(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}n_{2}}}+\frac{d\log(1/t^{*})}{\gamma(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}n_{2}}\right)=R(n_{2},(t^{*})^{1/\gamma})

where
C
is a universal constant. Hence,
(A2-ratio)
holds with
C_{\mathtt{ratio}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{ratio}}
by Markov’s inequality.

Verifying
(A4-ratio)

Similarly for any
\delta>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\E_{P^{2}}^{*}\left[\sup_{d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}}{n_{2}^{2}}\left|\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{2}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}]}{\{\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})C}{(t^{*})^{2/\gamma}n_{2}}\left(\frac{(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}d\log(1/t^{*})}{\gamma n_{2}}+\left(\frac{d\log(1/t^{*})}{\gamma n_{2}}\right)^{2}\right)=S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},(t^{*})^{1/\gamma},\alpha),

and

\displaystyle\sup_{d_{\Delta}(\theta,\theta(P^{N}))>(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}}\frac{z_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})^{2}(Z_{i})]}{n_{2}^{2}\{\M_{2}(\theta)-\M_{2}(\theta(P^{N}))\}^{2}}\leq\frac{(1+z_{\alpha}^{2})}{(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}n_{2}}=S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},(t^{*})^{1/\gamma},\alpha),

and
(A4-ratio)
holds with
\widetilde{C}_{\mathtt{emp}}=1/\varepsilon_{\mathtt{emp}}
by Markov’s inequality.

We assume that
n_{2}
is large enough satisfies the following:

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}\max\{R(n_{2},\rho),S_{\mathtt{emp}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha),S_{\mathtt{pop}}(n_{2},\rho,\alpha)\}\leq 1/3,

(E.16)

where
C_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
is a constant depending on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
. Then (
E.16
) is satisfied for
n_{2}
large such that

C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)^{2}\frac{d\log(1/t^{*})}{\gamma(t^{*})^{1/\gamma}}\leq n_{2},

where
C^{\prime}_{\varepsilon^{\circ}}
is a constant depending on
\varepsilon^{\circ}
.

Evaluating the rate of convergence.

Throughout, let
\mathfrak{C}
denote a constant, depending on
C_{0},\gamma
, that may change from line to line. First, we obtain the value related to
\phi_{n_{2}}
, that is,

\displaystyle r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\phi_{n_{2}}(\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})\leq 1

\displaystyle\quad\Leftrightarrow r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\sqrt{\frac{\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)}d\log(1/\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})}{n_{2}}}+r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\frac{d\log(1/\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})}{n_{2}}\leq 1

\displaystyle\quad\Leftarrow r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\sqrt{\frac{\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)}d\log(1/\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})}{n_{2}}}\leq 1/2\quad\textrm{and}\quad r_{n_{2}}^{-2}\frac{d\log(1/\mathfrak{C}r_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})}{n_{2}}\leq 1/2.

The first and the second inequalities give

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{C}d}{n_{2}}\leq r_{n_{2}}^{(2+4\gamma)/(1+\gamma)}\log(1/r_{n_{2}})\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{\mathfrak{C}d}{n_{2}}\leq r_{n_{2}}^{2}\log(1/r_{n_{2}}).

Solving these inequalities assuming
d\leq n_{2}
, we arrive

\displaystyle r_{n_{2}}\geq\mathfrak{C}\left(\left(\frac{d\log(n_{2}/d)}{n_{2}}\right)^{(1+\gamma)/(2+4\gamma)}+\left(\frac{d\log(n_{2}/d)}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\Leftrightarrow r^{2/(1+\gamma)}_{n_{2}}\geq\mathfrak{C}\left(\left(\frac{d\log(n_{2}/d)}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/(1+2\gamma)}+\left(\frac{d\log(n_{2}/d)}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/(1+\gamma)}\right).

The first term is always larger then the second for
n_{2}\leq d
.
Next, we evaluate the value related to
\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}
, which yields,

\displaystyle u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\omega^{2}_{\mathtt{pop}}(\mathfrak{C}^{-1/(1+\gamma)}u_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)})\leq n_{2}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow u_{n_{2}}^{-4}\mathfrak{C}^{-1/(1+\gamma)}u_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)}\leq n_{2}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\mathfrak{C}^{-1/(1+2\gamma)}n_{2}^{-(1+\gamma)/2(1+2\gamma)}\leq u_{n_{2}}.

Thus we can take
u_{n_{2}}^{2/(1+\gamma)}=\mathfrak{C}n_{2}^{-1/(1+2\gamma)}
, but this is bounded up to a constant by the term coming from
r_{n_{2}}
. Hence
Theorem

13
holds with

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}=\left(\frac{d\log(n_{2}/d)}{n_{2}}\right)^{1/(1+2\gamma)}+\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}^{1/(1+\gamma)}.

Next, from
(A1-global)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}_{N,\alpha}^{\mathtt{CLT}}=C_{\rho}^{-1}\left((1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}\right)=\frac{\mathfrak{C}(1+|z_{\alpha}|)\widetilde{s}_{n_{1},n_{2}}}{t^{*}}.

Finally, we relate this result to
\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|
using
(B5)
. The result established thus far and
(B5)
together imply that for any
\theta\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}^{\mathtt{Manski}}_{N,\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\|\theta-\theta(P^{N})\|_{2}\leq c_{1}^{-1}\mathfrak{C}d_{\Delta}(\theta_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\leq

with high probability in view of in view of
Theorem

30
. This proves the result.

∎

For
Corollary

24.1
, we instead prove the slightly rephrased version of the corollary.

Corollary 40.1
.

Proof of
Corollary

40.1
.

Next, we verify
(A5)
. First, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\E_{P_{i}}[\widehat{\xi}_{i}^{2}|D_{1}]\leq\frac{1}{n_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}d_{\Delta,i}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))=\frac{1}{n_{2}}d_{\Delta}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\leq C_{1}^{2}\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}.

A straightforward calculation gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}^{2}_{2}(\widehat{\theta}_{1})\leq d_{\Delta}(\widehat{\theta}_{1},\theta(P^{N}))\leq C_{1}^{2}\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{2}

Unfortunately, this is not sufficient and we need a stronger regularity condition. Observe that

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}(\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i})\neq\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i})

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i}\cdot\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}\leq 0

\displaystyle\Rightarrow|\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}|\leq|\widehat{\theta}_{1}^{\top}X_{i}-\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}|

\displaystyle\Rightarrow|\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X_{i}|\leq\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|\|X_{i}\|.

Hence

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}(m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\E_{P_{i}}\left[Y\left(\mathrm{sgn}(\theta(P^{N})^{\top}X)-\mathrm{sgn}(\theta^{\top}X)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{A}(\theta)}\,|\E_{P_{i}}[Y|X=x]|P_{X}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{A}(\theta)}\,|\eta_{P_{i}}(x)-1/2|P_{X}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{\mathcal{A}^{\prime}(\theta,x)}\,|\eta_{P_{i}}(x)-1/2|P_{X}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=2\|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})\|^{\gamma}P_{X}(\mathcal{A}^{\prime}(\theta,x)).

∎

S.7.4

Quantile without Positive Densities

Proof of Theorem
25
.

First, we establish the result for
\alpha\neq 1/2
as an application of
Theorem

8
. Consider the case when
\theta>\theta(P^{N})
, then

\displaystyle m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}

\displaystyle=\eta(X-\theta)_{+}+(1-\eta)(\theta-X)_{+}-\eta(X-\theta(P^{N}))_{+}-(1-\eta)(\theta(P^{N})-X)_{+}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta)(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{\theta<X\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{(1-\eta)(\theta-X)-\eta(X-\theta(P^{N}))\right\}\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}

\displaystyle=(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))(1-\mathbf{1}\{X>\theta(P^{N})\})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\theta(P^{N})-X+(1-\eta)(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\right\}\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}

\displaystyle=(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))

\displaystyle\quad+\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\theta(P^{N})-X+(1-\eta)(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\right\}\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}

\displaystyle=(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta(\theta-\theta(P^{N}))+(\theta-X)\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\theta\}.

Analogously, we have

m_{\theta}-m_{\theta(P^{N})}=\eta(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)-(\theta(P^{N})-\theta)\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}+(X-\theta)\mathbf{1}\{\theta<X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}

when
\theta(P^{N})>\theta
. Taking expectations, we obtain

\displaystyle\E_{P_{i}}[m_{\widehat{\theta}_{1}}(X_{i})-m_{\theta(P^{N})}(X_{i})|D_{1}]

\displaystyle\quad=\E_{P_{i}}[(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-X)\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\widehat{\theta}_{1}\}|D_{1}]+\E_{P_{i}}[(X-\widehat{\theta}_{1})\mathbf{1}\{\widehat{\theta}_{1}<X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}|D_{1}].

We now define

\displaystyle\widehat{\xi}_{i}=m_{\widehat{\theta}_{1}}(X_{i})-m_{\theta(P^{N})}(X_{i})-\E_{P_{i}}[m_{\widehat{\theta}_{1}}(X_{i})-m_{\theta(P^{N})}(X_{i})|D_{1}],

and take
G_{i}=\mathbf{1}\{X_{i}\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta
. For
\widehat{\theta}_{1}>\theta(P^{N})
and
|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})|\leq\delta_{0}
, under which
(B7)
holds, it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[|\widehat{\xi}_{i}-(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}))\cdot(\mathbf{1}\{X_{i}\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta)|^{2}|D_{1}]}{|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})|^{2}\E_{P_{i}}[(\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta)^{2}|D_{1}]}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[|(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-X)\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\widehat{\theta}_{1}\}|D_{1}]-\mathbb{E}_{P_{i}}[(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-X)\mathbf{1}\{\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\widehat{\theta}_{1}\}|^{2}|D_{1}]}{|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})|^{2}\E_{P_{i}}[(\mathbf{1}\{X\leq\theta(P^{N})\}-\eta)^{2}|D_{1}]}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{(\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N}))^{2}\mathbb{P}_{P_{i}}(\theta(P^{N})<X\leq\widehat{\theta}_{1})}{|\widehat{\theta}_{1}-\theta(P^{N})|^{2}\eta(1-\eta)}
